# OmniFlow — Complete Project Handoff Document
_Dated: 2026-09-04 · Status: PRODUCTION CORE COMPLETE — portal-driven AI bot live end-to-end_
_How to use: paste this whole document into a new chat as the first message. It is fully self-contained._

---

## 1. Project Overview

**OmniFlow** is a multi-tenant SaaS platform: businesses connect a WhatsApp number + AI agent, manage conversations, edit their business profile (products/prices/policies/FAQs) and configure the assistant's behaviour — all from a customer-facing **Portal**. The real WhatsApp connection stays on the owner's home laptop (distributed connector-node). A **Control Plane API** owns all tenant data; the **Website** (marketing + portal + admin) talks to it server-to-server.

- **User/owner:** Ahmed (laptop: Windows 11, CMD, venv Python bot at `C:\Users\Ahmed Ansari\Desktop\whatsapp-ai-bot\`). Communicates in **Roman Urdu/English mix** — reply in the same style. User wants **fast pace ("big moves")**.
- **Cost model:** strictly zero-cost — **Vercel Hobby + Neon Free**. No Render/Koyeb. Railway only if Vercel fails. No fake billing.
- **STANDING RULES:** all shipped UI/code text = professional English only (no Roman Urdu in placeholders/labels). No DB secrets/keys in chat. Never re-apply migrations. WhatsApp connector runs ONLY on the laptop.

---

## 2. Architecture (all live & verified)

```
[Customer WhatsApp] ⇅
[CONNECTOR NODE — Ahmed's laptop: Python bot (run_channel.py 1)]
   ├─ WhatsAppWebAdapter (Playwright DOM automation, session whatsapp_sessions/account_1)
   ├─ AI Router: Groq → Cerebras → Gemini failover (Cerebras quota exhausted → skip)
   ├─ ControlPlaneBridge (src/control_plane_bridge.py): status + ingest in/out + commands poll (15s) + 60s heartbeat
   ├─ portal_agent_config.py: GET /connector/bot every 60s (agent_name/tone/greeting/fallback/hours/handoff/CUSTOM INSTRUCTIONS)
   ├─ portal_business_profile.py: GET /connector/profile every 120s (products/prices/policies/FAQs)
   └─ System prompt = PORTAL_SYSTEM_PROMPT(): OWNER INSTRUCTIONS top priority → BUSINESS PROFILE → rules
        (hardcoded personas DELETED from ai.py + gemini_ai.py + prompt_builder v2)
        ▼ HTTPS X-Omniflow-Key (= OMNIFLOW_SERVICE_KEY, .env + oflow_service_key.txt)
[CONTROL PLANE — Flask on Vercel] https://omniflow-control-plane-rho.vercel.app (/api/v1)
   ├─ portal_auth (JWT cookies via /auth/me in-process), portal_db (Neon, lazy tables)
   ├─ portal_bot/ profile/ apikeys/ conversations/ channels/ admin_users/ auth_password_reset
   └─ connector_api: /whatsapp/status|commands|commands/ack|messages, /bot, /profile
        ▲ BFF (server routes, OMNIFLOW_CONTROL_PLANE_URL, no-store)
[WEBSITE — Next.js on Vercel] https://omniflow-bice.vercel.app
   ├─ Portal: Overview / WhatsApp setup / **Configure AI** (renamed from "AI agents") /
   │   Conversations / Business profile (About/Products/Policies/FAQs textareas) / Settings
   └─ Admin (Supabase session) + marketing site
```

**Navigation split (user's design):** Business profile = business data; Configure AI = assistant behaviour. Bot reads BOTH.

## 4. Environment Variables & Secrets

**Website:** `OMNIFLOW_CONTROL_PLANE_URL` (no /api/v1 suffix), `OMNIFLOW_SERVICE_KEY` + Supabase vars (admin/CMS).
**Backend:** `DATABASE_URL` (Neon), `OMNIFLOW_ADMIN_API_KEY`, `OMNIFLOW_SERVICE_KEY` (= laptop key), `OMNIFLOW_CONNECTOR_USER_EMAIL`=`12345@gmail.com` (portal login email, NOT admin email).
**Laptop .env:** DB_* (Neon), `OMNIFLOW_SERVICE_KEY` (auto-injected from oflow_service_key.txt), WHATSAPP_ACCOUNT_ID=1, voice flags. Optional bridge env: OMNIFLOW_CP_BASE_URL, OMNIFLOW_CLIENT_ID (default 1), OMNIFLOW_COMMAND_POLL_SECONDS(15), OMNIFLOW_AGENT_CONFIG_SECONDS(60), OMNIFLOW_PROFILE_SECONDS(120).

## 6. Key API Contracts (stable)

- **Connector** (X-Omniflow-Key = SERVICE/ADMIN key; tenant via explicit client_id or env email): `POST /whatsapp/status {state,phone?,account_name?}`; `GET /whatsapp/commands?limit`; `POST /whatsapp/commands/ack {command_id,ok,note?}`; `POST /whatsapp/messages {messages:[{from,body?,name?,direction in|out}]}` (1–100); `GET /bot` → flat config incl. `custom_instructions`; `GET /profile` → `{profile:{...},updated_at}`.
- **Portal BFF** routes under `/api/omniflow/portal/*` (HttpOnly cookies of_access_token/of_refresh_token; no-store; origin 403 on mutations).
- **ofk_ API keys:** SHA-256 stored, plaintext once, revoke sets revoked_at; read-only principal.
- **Bot config JSON (both /portal/bot and /connector/bot):** agent_name(1-64), tone(friendly|professional|concise), greeting(≤2000), fallback(≤2000), working_hours_enabled/start/end, human_handoff_enabled, **custom_instructions(≤2000)**, updated_at.
- **DB:** portal_whatsapp_status, portal_bot_configs (has custom_instructions), portal_connector_commands, portal_profiles, portal_api_keys, portal_conversations, portal_messages, platform_users (+client_id col added), user_password_credentials, password_reset_tokens. Migrations 001–008 applied; NEVER re-run. 008 tables lazily self-create.

## 8. Working Rules for the New Chat

- CMD-safe one-block-at-a-time commands; user pastes full output back.
- Any file delivery: self-verifying patcher scripts (anchor-check 1x, backup, py_compile/esbuild gate, auto-restore, idempotent, **CRLF-tolerant**) — scripts always pasted as chat code blocks; never ask user to hand-edit.
- Python patchers tolerate CRLF; JS patchers MUST normalize `\r\n` first.
- Commit scripts to `connector-bridge/` in the workspace repo after each delivery (GitHub push from sandbox is blocked; laptop pushes repos).
- All UI text professional English. Roman Urdu only for chat conversation.
- The bot's AI prompts must stay portal-driven: OWNER INSTRUCTIONS first, BUSINESS PROFILE second, then safety rules. Never reintroduce hardcoded personas.

## 28. HOTFIX: DEEP BFF ROUTE IMPORT DEPTH (Vercel module-not-found) — pending user run
SYMPTOM: Vercel build failed "Module not found" on app/api/omniflow/portal/broadcasts/preview/route.ts + csat/summary/route.ts (3 imports each, lines 1/2/6). ROOT CAUSE (Phase 9 add_growth_ops.mjs): those two NEW_FILE templates copied the import block of broadcasts/route.ts, which sits ONE level shallower; 5x ../ resolves to app/lib/... (nonexistent) instead of repo-root lib/. Exactly 6 broken imports in the whole tree (scanner over 339 relative imports; kb/gaps, kb/gaps/[id], conversations/[id]/csat all correct). FIX: fix_deep_imports.mjs (bot root) repairs the 6 imports (prefix 5x -> 6x ../), idempotent, *.pre_fix.bak backups, ends with a built-in FULL-APP resolution check (every relative import in app/ + lib/ must resolve; 339 checked). Source patcher add_growth_ops.mjs templates corrected too (repo + connector-bridge copies). VERIFICATION: sandbox full `npm install && npm run build` on Next 16.3.1: with fix -> "Compiled successfully" + TypeScript pass + 65/65 pages (homepage prerender timeout is sandbox-only: dummy CP URL + blocked network; Vercel reaches real CP); with fix reverted -> byte-identical Vercel error reproduced ("Can't resolve '../../../../../lib/omniflow/control-plane'" at route.ts:1:1). LESSON (validation battery update): esbuild WITHOUT --bundle never resolves imports — syntax only. Every future phase MUST run (1) the relative-import resolution scan and (2) a full `next build` (fonts stubbed offline) before delivery. Deploy: node fix_deep_imports.mjs -> commit the 2 route files in Omniflow -> push -> Vercel redeploys green. NO Control-Plane/bridge change, NO restart.

## 41. VERCEL BUILD REPAIR: DUPLICATE ANALYTICS BLOCK IN portal.ts (hotfix, pending user run)
fix_portal_duplicate.mjs (bot root, 1 edit, idempotent, backup .pre_dupfix.bak). INCIDENT: after the laptop's Phase-23 push (commit 8b48061 "feat: Auto-assign new chats to least-loaded teammate" - a full-tree squashed push), Vercel `next build` failed with an esparse error at lib/omniflow/portal.ts:3158 ("Multiple exports with the same name getAnalytics"). ROOT CAUSE: the laptop's portal.ts contained the ENTIRE analytics section TWICE (6 interfaces AnalyticsTotals/Window/DayPoint/Intent/TopEntry/Data + getAnalytics) - copy 1 inside the conversations region (line 1214/1256), copy 2 between the automations section and updateConversationStatus (lines 3116-3225). Duplicate top-level exports are illegal in an ES module -> build fails. Sandbox chain-reproduction from 38c9851 did NOT reproduce it (sandbox esbuild+tsc passed), so the dup came from the laptop's own earlier patcher history; found by extracting the exact committed tree (git archive 8b48061) and running esbuild locally. FIX: fix_portal_duplicate.mjs locates the LAST "export interface AnalyticsTotals {" before "export type ConversationStatusResult =" (single-copy marker after the dup), verifies the segment contains the dup getAnalytics + AnalyticsData and not the marker, cuts it, then post-checks all 7 analytics symbols are single before writing. VALIDATION: on the extracted laptop tree: 1 applied (3331 -> 3204 lines, 3531 chars removed), rerun 0/1/0, esbuild portal.ts OK, FULL tsc --noEmit exit 0, whole-tree duplicate-export scan = 0 files. Deploy: node fix_portal_duplicate.mjs -> Omniflow commit (lib/omniflow/portal.ts) -> push -> Vercel rebuilds green. NO restart, NO Control Plane change. Verify: Vercel deployment succeeds; Analytics page still renders numbers; Automations page cards intact.

## 44. PHASE 26: SERVICE LEVEL METRICS ON ANALYTICS (pending user run)
add_service_metrics.mjs (bot root, 8 swaps across 3 files, no new files, REQUIRES Phase 25 applied first). Zero AI, no bridge changes -> NO bot restart. Adds the two industry-standard service metrics to GET /portal/analytics + the Analytics page: RESOLUTION RATE (chats created in the selected window that are now closed: COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status='closed') / COUNT(*), % 1dp, None when no chats) and FIRST RESPONSE TIME (per conversation: first inbound -> first outbound STRICTLY AFTER it, correlated MIN(o.created_at) WHERE o.created_at > i.first_in; avg + true median via PERCENTILE_CONT(0.5), rounded 1dp, None when zero answered; unanswered first-messages excluded via WHERE t.out_ts IS NOT NULL). CP portal_analytics.py: 2 new window queries after entry_rows (service_rows + frt_rows; params (client, days) each) + compute block (window_created/closed, _round_seconds Decimal-safe) + "service": {resolution_rate, frt_avg_seconds, frt_median_seconds, answered_conversations} between window and per_day. lib: AnalyticsService interface + svc/svcNum extraction in getAnalytics + service mapping. AnalyticsClient: formatSeconds helper (s / m s / h m, em-dash when null) + "Service level" section (3 tiles: Resolution rate, Avg first response, Median first response with answered-count sub-label) between automation tiles and intents; optional-chained data.service so old cached payloads can't crash. VALIDATION (baseline origin/main 8b48061 + dup-fix website + 28-chain CP + Ph24/25): fresh 8/0/0, rerun 0/8/0, clean-state proof (restore 3 backups -> greps 0 -> 8/0/0), py_compile, imports 19/19, functional 29/29 (401/503 zero-queries; days=8 400; happy path 65.0%/125.4s/88.0s/9 + 10 executes + SQL fragments + params; empty window -> all None/0; structural), esbuild 3/3, resolution 387/0, tsc --noEmit exit 0. ## 46. PHASE 28: OVERDUE REPLIES (SLA ALERTS) + INBOX DEEP LINKS (pending user run)
add_reply_overdue.mjs (bot root, 20 swaps across 6 files, no new files, REQUIRES Phase 27 applied first). Adds timing to needs-reply: a chat is OVERDUE when the customer sent the last message and has been waiting longer than OF_SLA_HOURS (module const OVERDUE_HOURS in both portal_conversations.py and portal_growth.py; default 4, clamp 1..72, garbage falls back to 4). CP: list endpoint needs_reply filter now accepts 1|overdue (overdue adds HAVING MAX(CASE WHEN direction='in' THEN created_at END) < NOW() - make_interval(hours => %s) + OVERDUE_HOURS param before the limit param); export endpoint mirrors it with a distinct export_reply local (the first draft had identical to-texts for both swaps, so the list swap satisfied the export swap and the export endpoint stayed unpatched - caught in a debug rerun and fixed); overview subquery pair gains needs_reply_overdue (od/om aliases, 8 params = 7 clients + SLA) and stats.needs_reply_overdue. Website: OverviewData.needsReplyOverdue + mapping; listConversations(needsReplyFilter?: string) accepts 1|overdue (was boolean needsReplyOnly); BFF clamps the query value to 1|overdue; inbox gets a red Overdue chip (mutually exclusive with the amber Needs reply chip, shared replyFilter state+ref), export honours it, and the mount effect now seeds q and needs_reply from the URL (this also fixes the long-broken customer-to-inbox ?q= deep link); Overview shows "N overdue" as the Needs-reply tile sub-label and a red banner linking to /dashboard/conversations?needs_reply=overdue. VALIDATION (debug-instrumented rerun found the export skip; fixed): fresh 20/0/0, rerun 0/20/0, clean-state proof (restore 6 backups -> greps 0 -> 20/0/0), py_compile, imports 19/19, functional 27/27 (SLA const default/env/clamp/fallback; list overdue fragments + params (1,4,50) + 1/junk no-params; export overdue params (1,4,500); overview 8 params + counter; structural lib/bff/chips/url-seed/banner), esbuild 4/4, resolution 390/0, tsc --noEmit exit 0; regressions customer_message 32/32 + service_metrics 29/29. Deploy: node add_reply_overdue.mjs -> Omniflow commit (lib/omniflow/portal.ts "app/api/omniflow/portal/conversations/route.ts" "app/dashboard/(portal)/conversations/page.tsx" "app/dashboard/(portal)/page.tsx") -> OmniFlow-Control-Plane commit (portal_conversations.py portal_growth.py) -> archive. NO restart. ## 50. PHASE 32: LIVE CHIP COUNTERS (needs reply / overdue / unassigned) (pending user run)
add_chip_counts.mjs (bot root, 13 swaps across 4 files, no new files, REQUIRES Phase 31 applied first). GET /portal/conversations accepts include=counts and answers {"conversations": [...], "counts": {needs_reply, overdue, unassigned}} computed by one aggregate query over ALL open conversations of the client (COUNT(*) FILTER: needs_reply = last-in-id > last-out-id HAVING subquery; overdue = same AND MAX(in created_at) < NOW() - make_interval(hours => OVERDUE_HOURS) in the same HAVING; unassigned = assigned_to IS NULL; params (OVERDUE_HOURS, client_id)). Without include=counts nothing changes (no second query) so all existing tests stay green. Counts query failure degrades to zeros with 200. BFF always passes includeCounts=true and forwards {conversations, counts}; lib maps snake_case to ConversationChipCounts (camelCase) and returns {conversations, counts}; page keeps chipCounts state and the Needs reply (amber), Overdue (red) and Unassigned (sky) chips each show a count badge when > 0; Mine stays badge-free. VALIDATION: fresh 13/0/0 (first draft had lib-type anchored on "export type ConversationSummary" but it is an interface - fixed to the interface line), rerun 0/13/0, clean-state (restore 4 .pre_counts.bak -> 12/0/0 then 13/0/0), py_compile, imports 19/19, functional 22/22 (counts payload incl. params (4, 1) and open-only scope, empty row -> zeros, DB failure -> zeros + 200, no-include single query and no counts key, full row shape, structural lib/bff/page). esbuild 3/3, resolution 390/0, tsc --noEmit exit 0. Regressions: inbox_sort 17/17, overdue 27/27, csat_dist 22/22. Deploy: node add_chip_counts.mjs -> website commit (lib/omniflow/portal.ts "app/api/omniflow/portal/conversations/route.ts" "app/dashboard/(portal)/conversations/page.tsx") -> CP commit (portal_conversations.py) -> archive. NO restart. Verify: Needs reply / Overdue / Unassigned chips show live numbers that update on refresh and polling; numbers cover all open chats even when a filter hides them.

## 63. HOTFIX: STAR PINS CP ANCHORS (laptop run reported 2 warnings)
User run of Phase 39 on the laptop reported "cp-helper NOT FOUND" and "cp-select-export NOT FOUND" (31 of 33 applied). Root cause: the sandbox CP reconstruction differed from the real laptop file in exactly two spots - (1) the laptop OVERDUE_HOURS block from Phase 28 reads "except ValueError:" while the patcher expected "except (TypeError, ValueError):", and (2) the laptop export SELECT is the Phase 15 text ("..., tm.name AS assignee_name" with NO trailing comma, no needs_reply EXISTS column, followed by " FROM " + portal_db._q(portal_db.CONV_TABLE) + " c") while the patcher expected a list-style SELECT. DANGER: the missing cp-helper is the DEFINITION of _ensure_star_column while its CALLS did apply, so the pushed CP momentarily 503s every conversations list/export/detail request (NameError caught -> portal_unavailable) and export ORDER BY references c.starred before the ALTER ever ran. add_star_pins.mjs is now updated to the laptop-accurate anchors and was re-certified on a laptop-accurate sandbox state: fresh 15 applied (CP) / 15 already (new BFF file + website) / 0 warnings, rerun 0/30/0, all 13 suites 240 PASS, py_compile, imports 3/3, tsc exit 0. USER RECOVERY: re-run the SAME command "node add_star_pins.mjs" -> expect "2 applied, 31 already done, 0 warnings" (the two missing CP swaps now apply), then re-commit portal_conversations.py and push again; the first list request afterwards runs the one-time ALTER and everything returns. LESSON (standing): every CP anchor for a region not read verbatim this session must be taken from the original phase patcher that wrote it (add_reply_overdue.mjs, add_inbox_power_tools.mjs, ...), never reconstructed from memory.

## 90. PHASES 111-114: AWAY AUTO-REPLY ENFORCEMENT (pending user run - CP + BRIDGE + WEBSITE; BOT RESTART NEEDED)
The big parked feature is now END TO END. Flow: customer messages WhatsApp -> laptop bridge ingests to CP -> CP ingest hook checks business hours (enabled + closed now in the saved timezone via zoneinfo, weekday-math so no locale issues) -> if closed, enqueues ONE row in a lazy portal_away_replies table per conversation per 4h cooldown -> the bridge's poll picks it up (GET /connector/away-replies, blueprint prefix is /api/v1/connector so the decorators are just "/away-replies") -> sends via adapter.send(OutboundMessage) -> ingests it as direction=out so it shows in the thread -> acks (CP marks sent/failed). The connector blueprint's before_request service-key guard covers the new endpoints automatically (header X-Omniflow-Key).
add_batch_111_114.mjs (5 swaps, 3 files): Ph111 CP connector_api.py (helpers + endpoints inserted BEFORE the ingest decorator - the anchor includes @bp.post("/whatsapp/messages") so nothing dangles; ingest hook appended after the automations try/except, the true laptop tail verified from add_automations' TO). Ph112 bridge (src/control_plane_bridge.py): process_due_away + fetch/ack helpers, anchored on the followup-patcher TO blocks (run_due_commands head with the followups try + the followup print tail) - REQUIRES the followup agent batch on the laptop (it is). Ph113 channels page: amber AwayChip (self-fetching component appended after the main component; chip render before the footer note). Ph114 rig suite test_away.py (27 checks: closed-now math incl. bad-tz/wrong-days safety, enqueue vs cooldown vs direction vs disabled, 403 guard, list shape, ack 200/404/400, lazy DDL, structural). VALIDATION was done against SIMULATED laptop-accurate states (connector_api with the automations hook recreated; bridge with the REAL followup TO blocks applied): fresh 4 applied + 1 already (chip from an earlier sim), rerun 0, py_compile both, 25 suites 486 PASS, tsc exit 0, esbuild OK. RUNBOOK GOTCHAS learned: connector routes live under the /api/v1/connector blueprint prefix (decorators must NOT repeat it) and the rig's DbStub needs one scripted result per execute.

## 91. HOTFIX: P111 INGEST-HOOK ANCHOR (fixed patcher, laptop rerun needed)
The laptop's first run reported `p111-ingest-hook NOT FOUND`: my anchor assumed the automations hook sat directly before `inserted += 1`, but the laptop's ingest tail grew hooks in a different order (the automations patcher itself INSERTED the growth hook, and the KB patcher anchored on the same except/pass/inserted tail - so the true order on the laptop differs from my reconstruction). FIX shipped in add_batch_111_114.mjs: the hook swap now anchors on the single `inserted += 1` line (verified exactly one occurrence in the base file and every later patcher preserves it) with a CALL-SITE-SPECIFIC guard (`_maybe_enqueue_away_reply(` + newline + tenant line - a bare function-name guard would have been tripped by the away-block's own TO; caught and fixed in the sandbox). VALIDATED against THREE simulated laptop tails (no-automation, with-automation, with-kb): fresh 2 applied (away-block + hook), rerun 0, py_compile OK, test_away 27 PASS. Laptop: replace add_batch_111_114.mjs and rerun (expect 1 applied p111-ingest-hook, 4 already, 0 warnings - everything else from the first run persists). If the hook still warns, run `findstr /n "inserted += 1" OmniFlow-Control-Plane\connector_api.py` and paste the output.

## 92. PHASES 115-116: THE COUNTS-QUERY SPEED FIX (pending user run - CP ONLY)
User pasted no Network numbers yet, so the code-side slowest thing was hunted directly - and found: the chip-counts query ran THREE correlated EXISTS/GROUP-BY subqueries over the whole messages table (needs_reply, overdue, unread) on EVERY inbox view and every 15s poll. add_batch_115_116.mjs (3 swaps, CP only): Ph115 rewrites counts_sql as ONE pass - a "recent" CTE aggregates per-conversation last_in/last_in_at/last_out over a single messages scan (JOIN conversations on client+open), then needs_reply/overdue/unread become simple COUNT FILTERs over a LEFT JOIN; params become (client, hours, client); the chip_counts-pinned shape (make_interval(hours => %s)) is preserved and the unread COALESCE semantics are byte-equivalent. Ph116 adds idx_portal_messages_conv_dir (conversation_id, direction, id) via the existing once-per-process lazy migration (safe: IF NOT EXISTS; Vercel cold starts rerun it cheaply). VALIDATION: fresh 3 applied / 0 warnings first build, reruns 0, NEW test_counts_perf suite 13 PASS (single CTE, exactly one messages reference, no nr/od/nb correlated passes, params order, unread semantics, index present), chip_counts' two implementation-pinning checks updated to the new shape (unread COALESCE text, params (1,4,1)), 26 suites 499 PASS, py_compile OK. Expected effect: the heaviest recurring query drops from ~3 full message-table passes to 1 index-assisted pass - the dominant backend cost of every inbox load and every poll.

CP repo: git add portal_conversations.py; commit with message "perf: single-pass chip counts and messages direction index"; push. Archive add_batch_115_116.mjs. No website change, no restart. VERIFY: inbox loads and switches filters noticeably faster; chips still show correct numbers; overdue/unread counts unchanged. THEN send the Network-tab numbers (total requests/size/time + slowest XHRs) - they decide whether any further speed work is needed or the portal is done.

## 122b. HOTFIX: VERCEL BUILD FAILURE — CLIENT/ SERVER BOUNDARY (fix_client_boundary.mjs; pending user run - WEBSITE ONLY)
The 351-370 deploy broke the Vercel build: app/dashboard/(portal)/growth/page.tsx and conversations/[id]/AssistCard.tsx were "use client" components that VALUE-imported lib/omniflow/portal.ts, which transitively imports session-cookies.ts (next/headers) - Next.js refuses that in the client graph. tsc/esbuild do NOT catch this boundary (why the sandbox gates passed); only next build does - it is now a mandatory gate for any batch that ships client components. EVERY OTHER client component is safe (AnalyticsClient, FollowupSettingsForm, KnowledgeBaseClient, CatalogSection, OrdersSection all use `import type` which is erased). fix_client_boundary.mjs (3 changes): (1) growth/page.tsx rewritten fetch-first - all data via the existing BFF routes (churn/staffing/broadcast-suggestions/negotiation/settings+negotiation/quote/checkout/links/listen/rules/routing/rules), local interfaces, zero portal.ts imports; (2) AssistCard.tsx fetches /api/omniflow/portal/conversations/<id>/assist directly (BFF handles auth, so requirePortalAccessToken is gone from the client); (3) NEW BFF app/api/omniflow/portal/listen/hits/route.ts (GET, 6 ../ - the hits list the Growth page shows had no route). VALIDATION: fresh sandbox = 3 applied / 0 warnings, rerun 0/3; npx next build FULL PASS (134 pages) after the fix - reproduced the failure mode first to confirm the diagnosis; tsc 0; esbuild 3/3; 76 suites 2245 PASS / 0 FAIL (test_growth_ui grew to 107 checks with client-boundary pins: the two files must never import portal, plus fetch-URL and listen/hits depth pins). Sandbox-only note: the full build there needs NEXT_FONT hacks for Google Fonts (offline); fonts fetch fine on Vercel.

## 150. PHASES 831-850: PAYMENT GATEWAY (one patcher; pending user run - CP + WEBSITE; restart the CP service once after applying)
Item 4 of the 4-8 mega-round - the whole gateway in ONE batch (no phased rollout). Design decision: HOSTED-CHECKOUT HMAC flow with NO keys of ours - the gateway stays OFF until the owner stores THEIR OWN JazzCash/Easypaisa credentials in Settings; sandbox toggle switches provider sandbox hosts. CP: NEW portal_payments.py - portal_payment_settings(client_id PK, provider, enabled, sandbox, merchant_id, password, salt, store_id, timestamps) + portal_payment_intents(id, client_id, token UNIQUE, intent_id, provider, amount, status pending|paid, created_at; paid -> paid is single-use). GET /portal/payments/settings masks secrets (**** + last4); PUT is human+money guarded; GET /portal/payments/intents = last 20. /public/checkout/<token>/payinfo (no auth): enabled = configured AND enabled AND link status='open' AND due>0, due = round(total-paid,2), unknown token -> {enabled:false,due:0} 200. /public/checkout/<token>/pay builds the provider form: JazzCash pp_Version 1.1 / pp_TxnType MWALLET / pp_Amount = due*100 / HMAC-SHA256(salt, sorted "&"-joined params) uppercase; Easypaisa storeId + hash algo; auto-submitting HTML form to the sandbox or live host. POST /public/payments/callback/<intent> VERIFIES THE HMAC FIRST (bad -> 403), then credits paid_amount += amount, auto-flips the link status open -> paid, intent -> paid, audit payment.gateway_paid. WEB: getPaymentSettings/savePaymentSettings/getPublicPayInfo/fetchPublicPayHtml clients; payments/settings BFF (GET+PUT) + public checkout/[token]/pay BFF (streams the provider form, no-store); /c/[token] shows an emerald "Pay <due> online" button ONLY when payinfo.enabled; Settings gains PaymentsCard (provider select, enable + sandbox toggles, credential inputs, masked readout, save -> refetch).
VALIDATION: test_payments 23 checks incl. the HMAC VECTOR jazzcash_hash("testsalt123", {pp_Version:1.1, pp_TxnType:MWALLET, pp_MerchantID:M123, pp_Amount:50000, pp_Description:Order payment}) = D6C54E3A647D6B85D220416AC709231A0E92446FAD2F2FACBF525B2B73441944 (reproducible on the laptop); masking, money-guard on PUT, payinfo gating (unknown/disabled/paid), callback verify-first, single-use paid, auto status flip, audit. The patcher also carries the batch-1091..1141 idempotent repairs (11 export-symbol units + healApp + the drifted-bridge send_media fallback repair) as no-op safety. ## 170. HOTFIX - RECURRING VERCEL BUILD FAILURE FIXED AT THE ROOT (new tools/patchers/fix_portal_exports.mjs + batch 1091/1101 patchers regenerated; pending user run - WEBSITE only; no CP restart needed)
Root cause of every recurrence of "Export putAlertSettings doesn't exist": the OLD laptop repair patchers checked idempotence by START MARKER only (insertClientBlock keyed on "export interface PortalAlert {"). A laptop that once ran a TRUNCATED alerts block (the original extraction bug: interface + AlertsPayload + listAlerts present, markAlertsRead + putAlertSettings missing) made every later run - including the FIXED batch 1091 patcher - report "block already present" and skip. The missing exports could never come back, so Turbopack kept failing with exactly that error while the B8 clients (putRecoverySettings) were present - which is also why the error suggested "Did you mean putRecoverySettings?". FIX - repair at EXPORT-SYMBOL granularity (new MARKER-LAW amendment: a repair op must verify its OWN export line, never a block's start marker): the B4/B5/B6-client region is split into 11 disjoint units (GrowthBundlePayload iface, getGrowthBundle, PortalAlert iface, AlertsPayload iface, listAlerts, markAlertsRead, putAlertSettings, BrainSettings iface, BrainDraftResult iface, getBrainSettings, putBrainSettings); a unit is inserted ONLY if its own first export line is absent from the file, always immediately before "export async function replayDelivery(" - any partial state heals with zero duplicate symbols, a complete file is a strict no-op. Shipped as: (1) NEW standalone tools/patchers/fix_portal_exports.mjs (7.7 KB, backup .pre_fixexp.bak, 11 units); (2) add_batch_1091_1100_recovery.mjs regenerated - its marker-based repair ops replaced by the same 11 unit ops (ensureExportBlock added to the engine; 11 calls, healApp unchanged); (3) add_batch_1101_1110_risk.mjs regenerated from the fixed engine with the same 11 unit ops (78.0 KB). Repo sources NOT changed - only patchers. VALIDATION (all on exact laptop states built from the mimic): V1 = the user's exact error state (alerts truncated at listAlerts) -> fixer restores exactly 2 (markAlertsRead + putAlertSettings), 9 already, 0 warnings; rerun 0/11/0; tsc whole tree 0 (byte order may differ from repo on healed files - functionally identical; export presence + tsc is the heal contract). V2 = deep drift (all 11 units stripped) + NEW batch 1091 -> 11 applied / 17 already / 0 warnings, rerun 0/28/0, all six exports present, tsc 0. V3 = same drift + batch 1101 ALONE -> 11 applied / 11 already / 0 warnings, rerun 0/22/0, tsc 0. Fully-patched mimic: fixer 0/11/0, 1091 0/28/0, 1101 0/22/0, 0 warnings. Pinned suites re-run green: recovery 77, risk 57, growth_ui 218, memory 83 - 0 FAIL. ## 169. V2 B9 - COD INTELLIGENCE + ADDRESS INTELLIGENCE (one patcher add_batch_1101_1110_risk.mjs - includes the idempotent laptop repair; pending user run - CP + WEBSITE; restart the CP service once, redeploy the website)
Ninth V2 roadmap batch. CP: NEW portal_risk.py. RTO SCORE 0-100 - pure deterministic math over the shop's OWN data, no LLM in the score: base 35, + the return ratio of finished orders x30 (new customer +10), + the city return rate x0.25 (owner-curated portal_city_rates table, up to 50 cities, with a city_default_pct fallback), + order value bands (+8 above 20k, +12 above 50k), +/- COD confirm speed (fast reply -5, normal 0, slow 24-48h +8, very slow +12; no history +5). Every point lands in a factors list [{key, points, note}] - the WHY the owner sees, not just a number. Bands vs the configurable threshold (portal_risk_settings.score_threshold, clamped 50-95, default 70): below threshold-20 = "proceed", below threshold = "collect_advance", at/above = "hold" - and a portal_risk_tasks staff row is created ONLY when staff_tasks is on (ADVISORY-ONLY by default, which is also the rollback switch); one open task per contact (deduped), listed with Done, every creation audited as risk.task. ADDRESS INTELLIGENCE: normalize_address() cleans free-text Pakistani addresses (whitespace/comma collapse, hno/blk/sec/soc/moh/rd/st expansions), extracts the phone (03xx/+92 patterns) and the city (built-in 30-city list + the tenant's own cities), flags what is missing (no house number, no city, too short, no phone, empty) and returns the ASK FLOW - the exact Roman-Urdu questions to send the customer ("House ya street ka number bhi bata dein?", "Aap kis city me hain?..."). Endpoints (human-only, api-key 403): GET /portal/risk/score?contact=&total=&address= (address auto-extracts the city for the rate), GET/PUT /portal/risk/settings (threshold, staff_tasks, city_default_pct + full replace of the city table), GET /portal/risk/tasks + POST /portal/risk/tasks {id} (mark done), POST /portal/address/normalize. WEB: the COD page gains two cards above the requests list - RiskCard (contact + optional order value -> the score with a Proceed/Collect advance/Hold chip, the factor list with +/- points, threshold + staff-task toggle inline, open staff tasks with Done buttons) and AddressCard (paste -> cleaned address with copy, city/phone line, per-question Roman-Urdu chips with copy - ready to paste into the chat); four BFF routes; portal.ts clients getRiskScore / getRiskSettings / putRiskSettings / listRiskTasks / completeRiskTask / normalizeAddress (BEFORE replayDelivery, state-agnostic). app.py registers the bp (ensureAppLines with B4..B8 fallback chains). ## 168. V2 B8 - REVENUE RECOVERY + NEGOTIATION v2 + AI COPY-GEN (one patcher add_batch_1091_1100_recovery.mjs - includes the LAPTOP REPAIR; pending user run - CP + WEBSITE; restart the CP service once, redeploy the website)
Eighth V2 roadmap batch, and the patcher that also fixes the Vercel build. REPAIR FIRST (why the Vercel deploy failed): laptops that ran batch 1061 while their portal.ts was missing the B4/B5 client blocks pushed a lib/omniflow/portal.ts WITHOUT putAlertSettings / getGrowthBundle - Turbopack then failed with "Export putAlertSettings doesn't exist in target module". This patcher re-applies the B4/B5 app.py wiring (healApp: every missing rollups/obs/alerts line after the LAST existing import/registration, py_compile-guarded) and the FULL B4/B5 portal.ts client blocks (insertClientBlock before replayDelivery; block extraction is boundary-based so ALL functions inside are captured - the earlier fix_1061 cut the alerts block at listAlerts and missed markAlertsRead/putAlertSettings, both patchers are regenerated fixed) before delivering B8. One run heals the laptop AND ships B8; fix_1061_laptop_drift.mjs stays available as the standalone repair. B8 CP: NEW portal_recovery.py - (1) RECOVERY SCAN (lazy, runs when the dashboard asks, no background worker): abandoned checkout links (open > checkout_hours, default 24h), unconfirmed COD requests (pending > cod_hours, default 12h), price objections (recent "mehnga / mehngai / price zyada / kam kar / discount / ghat" inbound messages, 7-day window) and inactive high-value buyers (paid >= min_value, default 5000, quiet > inactive_days, default 21d) - detections land in portal_recovery_queue deduped by UNIQUE(client_id, kind, ref_key); FOLLOWUP_COPY is fixed Roman-Urdu per kind (never an LLM); auto_enabled queues the follow-up for NEW items itself (one per item ever - status open -> contacted) with a recovery.auto audit row; the owner can also press Follow up / Dismiss per item. (2) NEGOTIATION BOUNDS: owner sets min_price + max_discount_pct (portal_deal_settings); floor = max(min_price, price*(1-pct/100)) is COMPUTED, never asked; decide() is deterministic - offer >= price or >= floor: accept; within 5% below floor: counter AT the floor; below: reject - and the LLM only PHRASES the counter message, its text is DISCARDED unless it contains the exact formatted counter price (so the agent can never cross the bounds); every round logs to portal_deal_rounds + negotiation.round audit. Endpoints /portal/negotiation/bounds (GET/PUT) + /portal/negotiation/decide (POST) sit BESIDE the old /negotiation/settings + /negotiation/quote module (untouched; the B8 tables are portal_deal_settings/portal_deal_rounds so there is no schema clash). (3) COPY-GEN: POST /portal/copygen/broadcast {topic, lang ur|roman|en} returns two short variants; WITHOUT an LLM key it falls back to fixed per-language templates (urdu script / roman / english) so the feature always works; returns TEXT ONLY - the owner edits before sending (edit-before-send law). All endpoints human-only (api keys 403). WEB: RecoveryCard on the Overview under the Daily Brief (kind labels, Follow up / Dismiss, Auto on/off toggle), NegotiationCard in Settings under AI Brain (bounds form + a quick decide calculator showing accept/counter/reject with the floor and a ready reply), CopyGenCard on the Broadcasts page above the composer (topic + language, two variants with Copy buttons, fallback note when no AI key); seven BFF routes (recovery, recovery/followup, recovery/dismiss, recovery/settings, negotiation/bounds, negotiation/decide, copygen/broadcast - the B8 paths do not collide with the old negotiation routes); portal.ts clients under the PriceBounds naming (the old DealBounds/getNegotiationSettings/saveNegotiationSettings clients are untouched). app.py registers the recovery bp (ensureAppLines with B4..B7 fallback chains). Anchors per-op unique single lines; backups *.pre_b10911100.bak; idempotent. ## 167. V2 B7 - CUSTOMER MEMORY + JOURNEY + EXPLAIN (one patcher add_batch_1081_1090_memory.mjs; pending user run - CP + WEBSITE; restart the CP service once, redeploy the website)
Seventh V2 roadmap batch. CP: NEW portal_memory.py, three related things in one slim module. MEMORY: structured customer notes (kind = preference | note | fact) with full owner CRUD - add, inline EDIT, delete, and a one-click "Forget all" purge that also clears the contact's journey state (the data-safety switch; the API is human-only, API keys get 403, because customer memory is personal data). Capped at OF_MEMORY_CAP=50 entries per contact (oldest trimmed automatically); remember_fact() is the fail-silent, deduped append helper the automations use from B8 onward. JOURNEY: configurable stages per tenant - seeded New / Engaged / Customer on first use, owner can add more (up to OF_JOURNEY_MAX_STAGES=12) and deactivate unused ones (delete is refused with 409 while contacts still sit on the stage). Moving a contact writes portal_journey_state + a portal_journey_events row AND a journey.stage portal_action_log event - transitions-as-events on the same audit bus webhooks already listen to. THE AUTOMATION: a FULLY PAID checkout link auto-advances the contact to the "Customer" stage (the advance endpoint's RETURNING now also returns contact_id; note_purchase is fail-silent, source "automation", and never moves a contact that is already there). EXPLAIN: GET /portal/explain?contact= returns the contact's last 15 portal_action_log rows (ai answers, journey moves, COD confirmations, broadcasts...) - the "why did this happen" card, populated straight from the audit trail. Endpoints (all owner-only): GET/POST/DELETE /portal/memory (DELETE ?contact= purges), PUT/DELETE /portal/memory/<id>, GET/PUT /portal/journey, POST /portal/journey/stages, DELETE /portal/journey/stages/<id>, GET /portal/explain. WEB: Customer 360 gains two cards - CustomerMemoryCard (kind select + add form, inline edit/save/cancel, per-entry delete, "Forget all" with confirm) and CustomerJourneyCard (stage chips - click to move, add-stage inline, "Last move: X (auto) - when", and the "Why this happened" activity timeline); both mounted in ProfileClient above the recommendations section. Six BFF routes (memory + journey + explain at 5 x ../, memory/[id] at 6, journey/stages at 6, journey/stages/[id] at 7 - the depth law). portal.ts clients: listCustomerMemory / addCustomerMemory / updateCustomerMemory / deleteCustomerMemory / purgeCustomerMemory / getJourney / moveJourneyStage / addJourneyStage / deleteJourneyStage / getExplain (inserted BEFORE replayDelivery, state-agnostic anchor). app.py registers the bp via ensureAppLines with B4/B5/B6 fallback chains. Anchors per-op unique single lines; backups *.pre_b10811090.bak; idempotent. ## 166. V2 B6 - AI BRAIN v1: TOOLS, POLICY, GROUNDING (one patcher add_batch_1071_1080_brain.mjs; pending user run - CP + WEBSITE; restart the CP service once, redeploy the website) + FIX 1061 (fix_1061_laptop_drift.mjs - run this FIRST if batch 1061 printed warnings)
Sixth V2 roadmap batch - the platform's first intent-driven agent. CP: NEW portal_brain.py. TOOLS (read-only, tenant-scoped, budgeted at OF_BRAIN_MAX_TOOL_CALLS=3 per decision): recent conversation messages, the contact's checkout links (status/total/paid), active knowledge-base search (title/content/keywords ILIKE), stored customer language. REASONER: the tool output IS the prompt's context - the system prompt forbids inventing orders/prices/dates/policies and requires JSON {reply, needs_human, confidence}; one portal_llm.chat_json call per decision (max_tokens 300). POLICY ENGINE (deterministic post-checks, the real safety net): vetoes refund/money-back promises (English + Roman Urdu), discount promises, delivery-date guarantees ("pakka kal"), AI/system meta talk, replies over OF_BRAIN_MAX_DRAFT_CHARS=700 or with more than 8 newlines - any violation forces a handoff instead of a send. DECISIONS: needs_human, confidence < OF_BRAIN_MIN_CONFIDENCE (default 0.6), policy violation or no LLM key => "handoff" (brain steps aside, the B3 keyword path still answers); otherwise "send". AUTONOMY per tenant (portal_brain_settings.autonomy): "off" = brain never runs (the rollback switch), "suggest" = DEFAULT (owner-requested drafts only, NEVER auto-sends), "auto" = the ingest hook may queue the reply itself. PROVENANCE: every decision writes a portal_brain_traces row (kind ingest_answer|draft, decision, grounding JSONB with tool ids/confidence/violations/reason) + an ai.answer/ai.draft/ai.settings portal_action_log audit row. INGEST WIRE: the brain gate sits in connector_api BEFORE the B2 KB gate and claims the one-reply chain as "brain" only when it actually queues a send_message (source "ai_brain"); it is FAIL-SILENT (any error returns None and the existing keyword path handles the message - a missing OF_LLM_API_KEY changes nothing). ## 165. V2 B5 - FAILURE ALERTS + DAILY BRIEF V2 (one patcher add_batch_1061_1070_alerts.mjs; pending user run - CP + WEBSITE; restart the CP service once, redeploy the website)
Fifth V2 roadmap batch. When a delivery DIES (the connector exhausted its retries on a COD confirmation, a checkout send, ...), the owner now gets an in-app ALERT instead of having to find a thread note. CP: NEW portal_alerts.py - a small portal_alerts table (kind, dedupe_key, severity, title, detail, is_read) + portal_alerts_settings (one row per tenant; a MISSING row means enabled) + the owner API: GET /api/v1/portal/alerts (50 most recent + unread badge count), POST /portal/alerts/read ({"id": N} or {"all": true}), GET/PUT /portal/alerts/settings (the per-tenant rollback toggle) - all human-only (API keys get 403). raise_alert is FAIL-SILENT (any db error returns None and never breaks the caller's transaction) and DEDUPED (an unread alert with the same kind+dedupe_key suppresses duplicates, so retries cannot flood the bell). alert_dead_command fires from portal_events.on_command_ack right after a command is marked dead (not while it is merely retrying) - severity "revenue" when the action is revenue-critical (cod/checkout/order/payment prefix), "normal" otherwise. Global kill switch OF_ALERTS=0. WEB: a bell with an unread badge in the dashboard sidebar footer (NEW AlertsBell.tsx - 60s poll, dropdown with recent alerts, "Mark all read", "Turn off/on" toggle wired to the settings API); three BFF routes (alerts GET, alerts/read POST, alerts/settings PUT - the read/settings routes sit one directory deeper, B1's depth lesson applied: 6 x ../ imports); portal.ts clients listAlerts / markAlertsRead / putAlertSettings; and the Daily Brief (dashboard home card) gains "Needs attention" (dead+failed deliveries from the deliveries counts + unread alerts) and "Recommended actions" blocks - deterministic rules now, the B6 AI brain makes them smarter later. app.py registers the alerts bp (ensureAppLines). ## 164. V2 B4 - GROWTH SPEED: ROLLUPS + OBS + ONE-CALL BUNDLE (one patcher add_batch_1051_1060_growth_speed.mjs; pending user run - CP + WEBSITE; restart the CP service once, redeploy/refresh the website)
Fourth V2 roadmap batch - the latency elaj the Vercel finding demanded. The Growth dashboard fired ~14 parallel BFF calls per visit, each a Vercel->laptop WAN round trip; this batch collapses them into ONE call. CP: NEW portal_rollups.py - GET /api/v1/portal/growth-bundle?days=N re-dispatches the SAME 14 owner endpoints in-process (forged request context per segment, caller's Authorization forwarded - zero duplicated SQL, zero drift; a failing segment degrades to null independently), embeds daily rollup TRENDS from the new portal_rollups table (messages in/out per day, revenue per day, paid orders per day - three grouped idempotent upserts recomputed on rebuild; no workers, "nightly" is simply the first rebuild after midnight), and caches the assembled bundle per (tenant, days) for OF_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS (default 45s) so repeat clicks cost one dict lookup. Rollback: OF_BUNDLE=0 makes the endpoint 503 and the page falls back to its original parallel calls; OF_ROLLUPS=0 skips trend writes; OF_ROLLUPS_TABLE renames the table. NEW portal_obs.py - every extension request gets a short trace id echoed as X-OF-Trace-Id, and slow requests (>= OF_SLOW_LOG_MS, default 400) log ONE single-line JSON record (trace_id/method/path/duration_ms/status) - a slow dashboard click becomes one grep on the laptop. app.py wiring via ensureAppLines (two imports, the bp registration, _install_obs(aux_app)). WEB: BFF route app/api/omniflow/portal/growth-bundle (GET, days parsed+clamped server-side), getGrowthBundle client in portal.ts (503 -> "bundle_disabled" sentinel), and the Growth page's load() now tries the bundle FIRST and maps its 14 segments into the existing state setters; on any miss it falls back to the original Promise.all path (kept verbatim as loadLegacy). VALIDATION: fresh run 6 applied / 0 already / 0 warnings, idempotent rerun 0/6/0, all 6 patched mimic files byte-identical to repo sources, py_compile clean, tsc whole tree exit 0, esbuild clean on all three touched TS files; new rig test_growth (35 checks - trace header, slow-log fields, rollup SQL pins, trend reader, bundle composition/cache/kill-switch/auth, web wiring pins); FULL SWEEP on the patcher's own output 107 suites 3276 PASS / 0 FAIL. ## 163. V2 B3 - ROMAN-URDU LLM INTENT, COD FIRST (one patcher add_batch_1041_1050_intents.mjs; pending user run - CP ONLY; restart the CP service once)
Third V2 roadmap batch - the platform's FIRST LLM layer, deliberately tiny. CP only - no web, no bridge, no app.py. NEW portal_llm.py: a stdlib-only OpenAI-compatible chat client (no SDK, no new infrastructure) - point OF_LLM_BASE_URL at any compatible endpoint (OpenAI, Groq, OpenRouter, a local Ollama), put the key in OF_LLM_API_KEY, model in OF_LLM_MODEL (default gpt-4o-mini); OF_LLM_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=6, OF_LLM_ATTEMPTS=1; the client is FAIL-SOFT - callers get None instead of exceptions and must fall back, so a missing key or an outage never blocks a customer message. NEW portal_intents.py: the Roman-Urdu intent engine. parse_cod_reply() comes FIRST (roadmap order): it labels replies to the COD confirmation ask as cod_confirm | cod_cancel | cod_later | cod_other - the LLM understands "haan confirm kardo", "agle hafte karna", "nahi bhai cancel"; the fallback path is the EXACT regex behaviour the COD flow shipped with, so a key-less deployment behaves identically to B2. classify() labels KB intents (order_tracking | shipping | appointment | general) with the LLM ("mera order kahan pohncha"), falling back to portal_kb.classify_intent keywords; portal_kb.classify_intent stays as the offline table (docstring updated). A small in-process TTL cache (OF_INTENT_CACHE_SECONDS=600, 500 entries) dedupes identical texts. Kill switches: OF_LLM_ENABLED=0 or OF_INTENT_LLM=0 -> pure keywords, zero cost, zero latency. Wiring: the connector KB gate asks portal_intents.classify (the one-reply law is UNTOUCHED - hooks still claim away -> cod -> kb, side hooks always run) and the COD reply branch asks portal_intents.parse_cod_reply with its except-branch pinned to cod_other. Cost bounds: COD parse fires only while a pending confirmation request exists; the KB classify sits LAST in the one-reply chain. VALIDATION: fresh run 5 applied / 0 already / 0 warnings, idempotent rerun 0/5/0, all 5 patched mimic files byte-identical to repo sources, py_compile clean; new rig test_intents (35 checks - client fail-soft paths, LLM-wins/invalid/None fallbacks, cache, kill switches, COD labels, wiring pins); FULL SWEEP on the patcher's own output 106 suites 3241 PASS / 0 FAIL. ## 162. V2 B2 - ONE-REPLY LAW + SECURITY PACK (one patcher add_batch_1031_1040_onereply_rate.mjs; pending user run - CP ONLY; restart the CP service once)
Second V2 roadmap batch. CP only - no web, no bridge, no app.py changes. (1) NEW portal_ratelimit.py - a fixed-window Postgres rate limiter: one row per bucket in portal_rate_limits (lazy DDL), the window RESTARTS on every hit (ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE with a CASE), rows prune themselves once the bucket goes quiet (count<=1 delete pass), every failure FAILS OPEN (a limiter outage must never block customer messages), limits are env-tunable (OF_RATE_INGEST_PER_MIN=240, OF_RATE_CHECKOUT_PER_MIN=60, OF_RATE_CHECKOUT_POST_PER_MIN=30, OF_RATE_TABLE) - the ONLY new module; no Redis, no worker, no new infrastructure. (2) ONE-REPLY LAW: portal_events.claimed_by column (in DDL + ALTER TABLE ... ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS) audits which automation answered; record_inbound/mark_inbound_done share the _event_key helper and note_claim stamps the winner. portal_kb.maybe_auto_reply, portal_cod.maybe_cod_flow and connector _maybe_enqueue_away_reply now return True exactly when they queued a customer-visible reply (None otherwise); portal_kb.classify_intent adds a transparent keyword-hint intent (order_tracking/shipping/appointment/general - B3's intent engine replaces the table without changing callers). The ingest chain runs the reply hooks in priority order away -> cod -> kb, the FIRST hook that replies claims the event (claimed_by=<name>) and the rest stay silent; side hooks (language detection, compliance, routing) ALWAYS run after the gate. (3) SECURITY PACK - rate guards inside the target functions only: connector ingest per tenant (ingest:<client_id>), checkout link view per token (checkout:<token>), coupon apply (coupon:<token>), change-request submit (changereq:<token>), payment callback (paycb:<intent_token>) - each returns 429 {"error":{"code":"rate_limited",...}} and fails open when the limiter is unavailable; public_checkout returns the 429 payload as a plain dict because the helper contract is (payload, status). VALIDATION: fresh run 9 applied / 0 already / 0 warnings, idempotent rerun 0/9/0; all 9 patched mimic files BYTE-IDENTICAL to the repo sources; py_compile clean; FULL SWEEP on the patcher's own output 105 suites 3206 PASS / 0 FAIL (new rigs: test_onereply 35 checks - intent hints, hook return contract, claim audit, wiring order, limiter maths, 429s; test_security 19 checks - 404-vs-429 ordering, fail-open, buckets-per-token, static pins on totals/coupon-revert/one-open-pending/intent-load). ROLLBACK: restore *.pre_b10311040.bak files; the table is inert without the code paths.

## 161. V2 B1 - EVENT CORE V1: IDEMPOTENT INBOUND + RELIABLE COMMAND QUEUE + DELIVERIES UI (one patcher add_batch_1011_1030_events.mjs; pending user run - CP + WEBSITE + BRIDGE; restart the CP service once + restart the bot)
First V2 roadmap batch (P0 reliability). CP: NEW portal_events.py - the portal_events table (event_type, idempotency_key, status received|done|failed|dead, attempts, next_attempt_at, error_code/message, payload, trace_id) + command extensions (attempts, next_attempt_at, provider_message_id, error_code/message, idempotency_key via lazy ALTER). Inbound messages now pass an idempotency GATE in the ingest loop (record_inbound): a provider message id dedupes FOREVER (unique partial index on pid:% keys), a sha256 content fingerprint dedupes within a 10-minute replay window (OF_EVENT_REPLAY_WINDOW_SECONDS) so webhook replays cannot double-process while repeat texts still work; the gate FAILS OPEN on any db error; mark_inbound_done closes the lifecycle row after the hook chain. Outbound: a FAILED bridge ack now schedules exponential backoff (30s base doubling to a 15m cap; OF_EVENT_RETRY_* envs; OF_EVENT_MAX_ATTEMPTS default 5 -> status dead + error_code max_attempts), success stamps provider_message_id (bridge plumbing ready; legacy adapters send none), and requeue_due_commands (called from the connector poll - NO worker thread, no new infra) flips due failed commands back to pending; BOTH poll endpoints (whatsapp + telegram) also skip commands whose next_attempt_at is in the future. Owner API: GET /portal/deliveries (status filter + per-status counts, human-only) + POST /portal/deliveries/replay (failed|dead -> pending, attempts reset). BRIDGE: acknowledge_command accepts and forwards provider_message_id when present. WEB: DeliveryRow/DeliveriesPayload/listDeliveries/replayDelivery clients; BFF deliveries GET + deliveries/replay POST (depths 5/6 - REPLAY IS ONE LEVEL DEEPER, the audit caught a 5-vs-6 guess); Settings gains the Deliveries card (status count chips, retry countdown, error lines, Replay button). ## 159. PHASES 991-1010: CLOUD API TEMPLATES + MULTILINGUAL KB (+ THE KB REGEX FIX) + LANGUAGE INSIGHTS (one patcher; pending user run - CP + WEBSITE + BRIDGE; restart the CP service once + restart the bot)
Provider parity + follow-ups round (user: "ye ho gyaa ab next do complete"). THREE features, ONE patcher add_batch_991_1010_cloud_lang.mjs (14 ops). CP: NEW portal_cloud.py - portal_cloud_templates(client_id, name, language, status, category; UNIQUE client+name+language) is a SNAPSHOT FED BY THE BRIDGE (POST /api/v1/connector/cloud-templates/sync, X-Omniflow-Key guard, replace-all per client with dedupe + cap 200 + audit cloud.synced); owner GET /portal/cloud/templates (APPROVED first) + POST /portal/cloud/send {conversation_id, template_name, language_code default en, parameters[]} queues action send_template (opt-out aware: telegram channel when tg:) and writes the outbound thread row ("Cloud API template") + conversation touch + audit cloud.sent so outreach shows in the chat like WATI sends; NO text fallback anywhere - an unapproved/mis-parameterized template is rejected by Meta and the failure lands in the command note. KB GOES MULTILINGUAL AND THIS BATCH FIXES A REAL LATENT BUG: portal_kb._normalize_text shipped with double-escaped regexes (r"[^\\w\\s]") so normalized text was garbage and KB keyword auto-replies COULD NEVER FIRE on any install; regexes fixed and a lang column added (lazy ALTER ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS lang TEXT DEFAULT 'auto' on the GET path, values auto|en|ur|roman, create/update/list carry it, _clean_entry_payload validates); NEW match_knowledge_base_lang = same 3-per-keyword scoring, ties prefer want-lang (tier 0) over auto (1) over other (2), falls back to the classic SELECT when the column is missing so the ingest hot path never breaks; maybe_auto_reply reads portal_contacts.stored_language and uses the lang-aware matcher. Customer insights: portal_analytics customer-analytics gains languages[] (portal_contact_lang GROUP BY lang ORDER count DESC LIMIT 6, empty [] on any error). BRIDGE: send_template_message (POSTs the Cloud template body to OMNIFLOW_WA_CLOUD_URL with OMNIFLOW_WA_TOKEN + optional OMNIFLOW_WA_AUTH_HEADER/OMNIFLOW_WA_AUTH_VALUE overrides), sync_message_templates (derives .../message_templates?limit=200 from the messages URL, pushes the snapshot to the CP), a send_template dispatch branch, and a 10-minute throttled sync at the top of each command poll. WEB: portal.ts KbLang type + KbEntry/Input.lang + mapper + both KB create/update bodies + CloudTemplate/getCloudTemplates/sendCloudTemplate clients + CustomerAnalytics languages; the KB BFF routes (kb/route.ts + kb/[id]/route.ts) now parse/validate lang (default auto); TWO new cloud BFF routes (templates GET, send POST, depth 6); the chat InteractiveCard gains a "Cloud API templates" section (name + language + "v1 | v2" parameters input + Send -> queue; also repairs the WATI card's Roman-Urdu helper text to English); KnowledgeBaseClient gains a Language select on entries, a language chip per row and a language filter; growth Customer insights gains "Customer languages" count chips (typed inline). Drift law held: app.py via ensureAppLines (4 lines, dev-order fallbacks); bridge ops are single-anchor lineOps applied to BOTH src/ and connector-bridge/ copies (missing copies skip WITHOUT warnings) with backup + py_compile guard + auto-restore; kb/analytics/portal.ts/KB-BFF diffs via applySwaps (unique-anchor swaps, compile guard on CP files); portal_cloud.py + the two cloud BFF routes + the three tsx are whole-file writes (writeNewRepair - older laptop copies backed up as *.pre_b9911010.bak).

## 157. PHASES 951-970: WATI PROVIDER TEMPLATES (one patcher; pending user run - CP + WEBSITE; restart the CP service once after applying)
Option-1 round (user: "abhi filhal number 1 complete do"; the SAVED CATALOG idea is EXCLUDED per user). WATI - the BSP most PK merchants start on - only delivers PRE-APPROVED templates for outbound, unlike the Cloud API session sender, so 891's sender is not enough for WATI tenants. CP: NEW portal_wati.py - portal_wati_settings(client_id PK, base_url, api_token, enabled, updated_at) + portal_wati_templates(id, client_id, name, data JSONB, created_at; idx client_id+name). GET/PUT /portal/wati/settings (validate_wati_settings: enabled needs a http(s) base_url + token >= 8 chars; token stored PLAIN for API calls but always returned MASKED ****last4; human guard; audit wati.settings). POST /portal/wati/sync calls GET <base>/api/v1/getTemplates (Bearer) via the module-level _fetch_wati_templates (tests stub it) and snapshots ONLY status=APPROVED rows (dedup, cap 200) - DELETE-all + INSERT per client (audit wati.synced "N approved"); disabled/not-configured -> 400. GET /portal/wati/templates = the snapshot. POST /portal/wati/send {conversation_id, template_name, parameters:[{name,value}]} - loads the scoped conversation (404; 400 no contact), requires enabled+configured (400), the template MUST be in the approved snapshot (404 "sync again"), then _send_wati_template POSTs <base>/api/v2/sendTemplateMessage?whatsappNumber=<contact> {template_name, broadcast_name:"omniflow_portal", parameters} (Bearer; result:true REQUIRED - rejection -> 502). On success the outbound row goes INTO portal_messages (direction 'out', sender 'WATI template', body "(template) <name>") + the conversation's last_message_preview/at touch - so the send shows in the thread - and audit wati.sent. API shapes verified against WATI docs (v2 sendTemplateMessage + result flag). WEB: WatiSettings + 5 clients (get/save settings, sync, list, send - parameters mapped to positional {name:"<n>",value} pairs) in ONE MUT-anchor swap; FOUR BFF routes (settings GET+PUT, sync POST, templates GET, send POST - all depth 6); Settings gains WatiCard (enabled toggle, tenant URL, password token input showing the saved ****mask, Save, "Sync approved templates" + approved-name chips); the chat InteractiveCard gains a "WATI templates" section (per template: parameters input "Value1 | Value2 | ..." + Send -> /wati/send) - the card file is versioned with a NEW marker so older laptop copies get backed up + upgraded (writeNewRepair).

## 156. PHASES 931-950: LAPTOP HEAL + ANALYTICS-DEPTH COMPLETION (one patcher; pending user run - CP + WEBSITE + BRIDGE; restart the CP service once + restart the bot)
Fixes BOTH laptop incidents in ONE idempotent run and completes ANALYTICS DEPTH on the laptop. INCIDENT 2 (user-reported, supersedes nothing): add_batch_891_910.mjs printed "? OmniFlow-Control-Plane/app.py :: anchor NOT FOUND (swap 1/2, anchor 523 chars, starts 'from portal_churn import bp...')" - ROOT CAUSE: the 891 app.py swap was a 523-char MULTI-LINE anchor (churn->interactive imports + register span), violating the app.py drift law; a drifted laptop app.py (any differing line inside the span, e.g. a missing datasafety registration from the 851/871 trouble) fails it while the 40-char prefix still shows "1 partial hits". THE FIX HERE: ensureAppLines checks each of the TEN registration lines (payments/analytics/datasafety/interactive imports + registers + payments public pair) ONE BY ONE against the file and inserts ONLY the missing ones - fallback anchors are era-original single lines proven to exist on the laptop (portal_churn import/register), with dev-order predecessors tried first so a healed file lands byte-identical to the dev tree. STALE-MODULE CLASS (the 851 incident, generalized): portal_payments.py, portal_analytics.py, portal_datasafety.py, portal_interactive.py all go through writeNewRepair - a file WITHOUT its marker is backed up to *.pre_b931950.bak and overwritten (compile-guarded); with the marker it reports already-done. Same repair for the insight BFFs (analytics depth's routes), the interactive BFF trio, the checkout status BFF, PaymentsCard, DataSafetyCard, InteractiveCard + ThreadClient wiring, the four portal.ts client blocks (SINGLE-LINE CheckoutShareMutation anchors - drift-safe by construction), the growth-page analytics line-ops and the laptop bridge's send_interactive pieces (line-ops with single-line anchors + compile guard; on the rig's bridge SIM the branch anchor legitimately fails - on the REAL laptop bridge both anchors exist, and if 891 already patched the bridge the marker skips it). RESULT: a laptop at ANY partial state of 831-911 comes out COMPLETE - payment gateway, analytics depth (repeat-buyer/LTV + product-wise sales), data safety, interactive WhatsApp, paid-status guard support.
VALIDATION (sandbox): incident-replay (stale analytics + stale interactive planted, 851's module write skipped, lines stripped) + heal = modules REPAIRED with backups, app.py re-healed byte-identical (removing the two interactive lines and re-running heals to EXACT dev-tree identity), everything else already-done; heal on a fully-patched tree = 0 applied / 20 already / 1 rig-sim-only warning; FULL SWEEP 3010 PASS / 0 FAIL / 100 suites; tsc 0; esbuild OK. ## 142. PHASES 671-690: CHECKOUT LINK EXPIRY + VIEW TRACKING (one patcher; pending user run - CP + WEBSITE; restart the CP service once after applying)
Stale COD links stay open forever and nobody knows whether the customer even looked. portal_checkout.py: _ensure_checkout_tables gains one idempotent ALTER (expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ, view_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0); create accepts expires_in_days (int 1-60 -> expires_at = NOW() + N * INTERVAL '1 day' via CASE WHEN %s IS NULL; 0/absent/None -> no expiry; out of range or non-numeric -> 400 'Expiry must be between 1 and 60 days.'); edit accepts expires_in_days with a DYNAMIC SET clause built in Python (0 -> expires_at = NULL i.e. 'No expiry', 1-60 -> interval, absent -> expiry untouched, else 400); the PUBLIC token read now also SELECTs expires_at/view_count, increments view_count (COALESCE(view_count,0)+1 WHERE token, committed) for OPEN links only, and 404s ('This order link has expired.') when an OPEN link's expires_at is past - paid/shipped/etc. links are never gated (history stays viewable); datetime compare with a string-fallback (except TypeError -> ISO string compare) because stubs return ISO strings while psycopg returns tz-aware datetimes; list SELECT + _public expose expires_at + view_count (public view pops only contact_id/id as before). portal.ts: CheckoutLink gains expiresAt/viewCount (create + list mappings parse them); createCheckoutLink/editCheckoutLink take optional expiresInDays and send expires_in_days. BFFs: the EXISTING create route (links/route.ts) and edit route ([id]/edit/route.ts) sanitize expires_in_days (create: absent/empty -> null else integer 1-60 else 400; edit: 0-60) and pass it as the new 5th client arg. Growth page: local CheckoutLink gains expiresAt?/viewCount?; isExpired/expiryLabel helpers ('Expired' / 'Ends today' / 'Nd left', open links only); the /c/token meta line appends ' - N views' (open, viewCount>0) and ' - <expiry label>'; the edit panel gains a 'Link expiry' select (Keep current '' / No expiry 0 / 3 / 7 / 14 / 30) wired through editExpiry state -> saveEdit body expires_in_days (null when Keep current).
VALIDATION: generated against the PRISTINE post-651 base (24 swap regions: CP 10, portal.ts 5, BFF create 1, BFF edit 1, page 7 - all difflib-verified byte-exact); fresh chain+611+fix+651 apply = 5 applied / 0 warnings, rerun 0/5/0, identity 5/5; NEW test_link_expiry 25 checks (ALTER columns, INSERT CASE + day params, create 0/61/'soon' -> 400, edit interval/clear/400/untouched, public gate expired-open 404 + string-expiry 404 + future/paid/no-expiry 200, increment open-only + COALESCE SQL, list + _public field exposure); test_checkout's public stub gained the increment slot ([[CREATED], []]); growth_ui 196; FULL SWEEP 2750 PASS / 0 FAIL / 90 suites; tsc 0; esbuild OK; FULL next build PASS.

## 135. LAPTOP INCIDENT: 531-550 CP SWAP FAILED ON DRIFTED portal_conversations.py - FIXED BY fix_vip_conversations.mjs (repair patcher; CP ONLY)
WHAT THE USER SAW: node add_batch_531_550.mjs reported '? OmniFlow-Control-Plane/portal_conversations.py :: anchor NOT FOUND (swap 1/4, anchor 152 chars, 1 partial hits)'. ROOT CAUSE: all FOUR vip swaps for portal_conversations.py were multi-line captured spans, and the laptop's conversations module has drifted (the 331/471 law turns out to apply to CP module files too, not just app.py/connector_api). applySwaps is all-or-nothing per file, so the CP file was left UNTOUCHED while portal.ts + InboxClient.tsx DID apply (2 applied, 1 warning) - the website side of 531-550 is already installed on the laptop. THE FIX: fix_vip_conversations.mjs (CP only, web untouched) installs the same four pieces with SINGLE-LINE anchor chains + pad lines: vip helper (VIP_THRESHOLD + _paid_order_counts) before def _tags_map (3 anchor fallbacks), include=vip parse after the include_counts line (2 fallbacks), vip list block before the counts init / after conversations.append(item) (2 fallbacks), vip detail block after the tags assignment (2 fallbacks) - probe-verified per piece, backup + py_compile gate + restore on fail, per-piece diagnostics. On an UNDRIFTED tree the result is BYTE-IDENTICAL to what 531-550 would have written; on a deliberately drifted tree (signature changed, lines shuffled, comment added) all four pieces still install via fallbacks and vip 9 + chip_counts 23 + counts_perf 13 all PASS. Rerun = already. NEW LAW: portal_conversations.py joins app.py + connector_api.py on the KNOWN-DRIFTED list - any future patcher touching it uses line-based single-line anchors, never multi-line spans. (A simulation lesson re-learned the hard way: a drift sim that renames a function parameter must rename the body references too, and str.replace without count hits every occurrence - two broken 'drifted' files produced two false alarms before the real test ran clean.)

## 133. PHASES 551-570: ORDER STATUS UPDATES ON CHECKOUT (one patcher; pending user run - CP + WEBSITE; restart the CP service once after applying)
Sequel to COD confirm: fulfillment statuses travel to the customer automatically. Checkout links gain "shipped" and "delivered" statuses (open/paid/cancelled unchanged), and when a merchant marks a link paid, shipped or delivered the CP queues a WhatsApp message to that contact through the connector command queue (portal_growth._send_command, source checkout_update, opt-out aware). add_batch_551_570.mjs (5 changes / 3 files + 2 new): portal_checkout.py gets STATUSES += shipped/delivered, a lazy portal_checkout_settings table (client PK, notify_enabled BOOL default TRUE, tpl_paid/tpl_shipped/tpl_delivered, probe-once DDL), GET/PUT /portal/checkout/settings (templates capped at 500, {name}/{title}/{total} placeholders, blank -> professional English defaults without {name} so nothing reads "Hi ,"), and a notify step inside update_checkout_link: the UPDATE now RETURNs token/contact_id/title/total, and for paid/shipped/delivered it loads settings (unreadable/absent table -> defaults, exception-safe), best-effort looks up the contact name from the newest conversation (empty-safe - zero rows -> ""), renders the body, queues the send + checkout.notified audit - ALL inside try/except so a notify failure NEVER breaks the status change (response carries notified: true/false). portal.ts: CheckoutNotifySettings + get/save clients. NEW BFF checkout/settings/route.ts (GET+PUT, six ../ like checkout/links - safeJson takes TWO args only, no headers arg). NEW growth/OrderUpdatesSettings.tsx (self-fetching card: updates on/off toggle + three template boxes + Save, self-hides when the endpoint is unavailable) rendered between the checkout and keyword sections; growth page also gains Paid->Shipped->Delivered buttons and shipped(sky)/delivered(violet) chip colors. TEST BUG CAUGHT: the first _contact_name did rows[0] on zero rows - IndexError silently skipped notify for contacts with no conversation; the suite's "unknown contact still notifies" check caught it before delivery.

## 125. PHASES 391-410: CHURN RADAR v2 — DETERMINISTIC CUSTOMER CHURN SCORING (one patcher; pending user run - CP + WEBSITE; NO restart)
The LAST not-built item from the gap doc (deeper churn prediction). add_batch_391_410.mjs (8 changes / 8 files). NO NEW TABLES - read-only scoring over data that already exists, sibling module to portal_reco. Ph391 NEW portal_churn.py: loaders (_load_recent_messages last 10 via msgs JOIN conv - core tables, no probe; _load_oldest_open_cart + _load_paid_links - portal_checkout_links probed), _parse_ts/_days_since (datetime|date|ISO-string safe, naive->UTC, future clamps to 0). Ph392 score_contact() FIVE SIGNALS: A quiet - last inbound >=21d +25, >=45d +35 "No message from them in N days" (never-spoken contacts NOT penalised); B unanswered - newest message is outbound and >=7d +15 "Your last reply is unanswered for N days"; C cold cart - oldest open checkout >=7d +20, >=21d +30 "Cart open for N days"; D spend drop - prior 30-60d orders vs last 30d: prior>=1 & recent=0 +15 "No orders in the last month", elif prior>=2 & recent<prior +10 "Orders slowed down"; E dormant repeat - >=2 paid orders ever, newest >=45d +20 "Hasn't reordered in N days". score=min(100,sum); tiers: >=70 at_risk, >=40 cooling, else healthy. Ph393 GET /churn/score?contact= (400 missing, contact capped 100, signals block carries raw values incl. nulls). FOUR SIGNALS in build_suggestions(): A repeat-coverage pool (the contact's own paid items seed siblings via co-purchase, the bought items themselves are NEVER recommended back), B chat mentions - catalog names found (case-insensitive substring) in recent inbound, +60 & min(hits,3)*5; names under 4 chars NEVER match ("pao" would hit "paood" - deterministic guard), C co-purchase - items in OTHER contacts' paid links that share at least one paid item with this contact, +40 & min(count,5)*5, reason names the shared item, D bestsellers - workspace-wide paid items with >=2 orders fill the rest, +20 & min(count,6)*5. ## 115. PHASES 221-235: SEQUENCES PRO — REPLY-AWARE FLOWS + SMART STEP CONDITIONS + STEP FUNNEL (pending user run - CP + WEBSITE; NO restart)
THE big one — sequences stop being dumb timers and behave like WATI's premium automations, fully deterministic: (1) a customer's reply auto-pauses their running series so the bot never talks over the human; (2) any step can carry "only send if no reply for N hours" so nudges self-skip when the customer is already engaged; (3) every send/skip is logged and shown as a per-step funnel. add_batch_221_235.mjs (8 changes / 8 files, 48 swaps — biggest batch yet): Ph221 CP lazy DDL — sequences.pause_on_reply BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE, steps.only_if_idle_hours INT NULL, NEW portal_sequence_step_log(id, client_id, sequence_id, enrollment_id, step_no, action sent|skipped, created_at) + idx (client_id, sequence_id, step_no). ## 108. PHASES 186-190: WEBHOOK TEST + RETRY (pending user run - CP + WEBSITE; NO restart)
Operational polish for the integrations story: merchants can prove an endpoint works before relying on it and rescue failed deliveries without waiting for the poll. add_batch_186_190.mjs (5 changes): Ph186 POST /portal/webhooks/<id>/test - client-guarded, fires a signed {"event":"ping","data":{"source":"omniflow","webhook_id":N}} at the endpoint RIGHT NOW via the same _http_post (5s timeout), then records the attempt as a delivery row (event 'ping', action_log_id NULL so the uq_webhook_delivery unique index never blocks, attempts=1, delivered_at per 2xx) and answers {ok, delivered, status_code, error} - a DOWN endpoint still answers 200 with delivered:false (the result IS the answer). Ph187 POST /portal/webhooks/<id>/deliveries/<did>/retry - webhook guard first, then the delivery row scoped by webhook+client (404 otherwise), already-delivered 400s, else the SAME synchronous attempt+update logic as the poll (success: delivered_at=NOW(); failure: attempts+1 + 2*(attempts+1)-minute backoff) so a dead delivery becomes retryable on demand. Ph188 portal.ts clients (testWebhook/retryWebhookDelivery with WebhookTestResult/WebhookRetryResult) before the ConversationStatusResult anchor + BFF POST routes. DEPTH LAW (finally measured, stop miscounting): ups = number of directories from app/api down, i.e. test route (webhooks/[id]/test) = 7 ups, retry route (webhooks/[id]/deliveries/[did]/retry) = NINE ups; sequences enrollments GET route = 7, its [eid] DELETE = 8. Ph189 integrations page (full-file rewrite - it is a 151_155 NEW file): "Send test" button per endpoint (busy-disabled, status note like "Test delivered (status 200)" / "Test failed: conn refused"), the delivery log rows get "Retry now" on undelivered entries and refresh the log. Ph190 test_webhook_ops 42 checks (test slot map = 4 DDL + select + insert = 6; retry = 7; guard order, ping payload, down-still-200, already-delivered 400, 404s, poll-filter intact pin, clients, depths, page pins).

USER REPORT (two Vercel WEBSITE build failures after B14): (1) app/api/omniflow/portal/kb/route.ts + kb/[id]/route.ts - "Property 'lang' is missing in type ... but required in type 'KbEntryInput'"; (2) lib/omniflow/portal-asset.ts - "Export controlPlaneBaseUrl doesn't exist in target module ... Did you mean to import listBrainTraces?". ROOT CAUSE: NOT the repo - the repo tree compiles clean (tsc 0 twice); the LAPTOP website repo is a patchwork of eras - its portal.ts is an old copy (has listBrainTraces, has lang-required KbEntryInput, but predates the controlPlaneBaseUrl EXPORT change and the B14 clients) and its kb BFF routes are older still (entries built without lang). FIX in add_batch_1171_1180_buildfix.mjs (ONE self-healing patcher, run order does not matter): ALL 41 batch-1161 ops re-shipped idempotently + 4 new whole-file ops - portal.ts (marker = listCourierProviders, only in the current file; replaces drifted copies => export + B14 clients restored), kb/route.ts + kb/[id]/route.ts (marker = the lang-aware parse line; replaces pre-lang copies), portal-asset.ts + media/[id]/download/route.ts (belt-and-braces sync) - plus the 2 B14 CP modules (compile-guarded). Every op is writeNewRepair: marker present = already done, absent = backup (*.pre_b11711180.bak) + replace from canonical source, so a drifted laptop converges to the repo state no matter what was or wasn't applied before. RECURRING-ERROR ROOT FIX (process): every batch from now on ships this way - whole-file marker-guarded ops for every file the batch touches or depends on, and the runbook says git add -A on the website repo (whole-folder add on the CP repo), because partial staging was the real killer across 154/175/177.
