## 195. MASTER-UPGRADE WORKFLOW ENGINE v1 (trigger -> steps runner on the poll loop + Workflows page; pending user run - WEBSITE repo; patcher add_batch_1371_1385_workflow_engine.mjs)

Audit-first: automations/sequences/recovery/winback/restock/followups sab pehle se apne apne tables par chal rahe the aur chalte rahenge (LIFT = unify at the record level, rebuild NAHI) - naya portal_workflows.py unhi engines ko steps ki tarah call karta he: conditions = portal_policy.evaluate (same rules JSON jo Rules hub/dry-run use karta he), actions = portal_actions.execute (15 registry actions, HIGH-risk = portal_approvals.create_approval gate -> run waiting_approval, owner ka 1/0 WhatsApp/inbox decision poll par resume karta he), messages = queue_whatsapp_message (portal_growth._send_command path), handoff = portal_growth._escalate_conversation, AI decision = portal_llm.chat_json (customer text `customer_data` JSON me, system prompt "untrusted / never instructions"), enrich = portal_intelligence.analyze. Koi background worker nahi - run_due_workflows connector poll par ride karta he (sequences ki tarah), ingest par maybe_trigger_message (inbound only, keyword whole-word, once_per_conversation default). TABLES (lazy DDL, tenant-scoped): portal_workflows (status draft|active|paused|archived, trigger_type/config, stop_on_reply, version, last_log_id cursor), portal_workflow_steps (step_no/kind/label/config JSON), portal_workflow_versions (har save snapshot), portal_workflow_runs (status running|waiting|waiting_approval|completed|goal_reached|stopped|failed, resume_at, approval_id, context, dedupe_key UNIQUE per workflow), portal_workflow_run_log (step-by-step outcome). TRIGGERS 8: message_received / contact_created (ingest) + stage_changed / cod_confirmed / cod_declined / payment_received / checkout_created (portal_action_log stream, actor_kind <> 'workflow' -> self-loop impossible; activation cursor = MAX(log id) taake purane events replay na hon) + manual (Run test). STEP KINDS 9: condition, branch (else = stop|skip|continue|goto step), ai_decision (question + fallback yes|no + else), action (args templated {name}{first_name}{text}{stage}{event}; contact_id/conversation_id auto; `_`-prefixed keys stripped = actor spoof block), wait (1 min - 7 days), approval (summary -> approvals inbox), handoff (user_id optional -> first teammate), goal, stop. Limits env: OF_WORKFLOWS_MAX 20, OF_WORKFLOW_STEPS_MAX 12, OF_WORKFLOW_RUNS_PER_TICK 10, 10 steps per tick per run. stop_on_reply: customer reply -> waiting runs stopped (ingest hook). ACTIONS registry +5 MEDIUM (add_conversation_tag, assign_conversation, assign_agent, set_pipeline_stage [actor_kind workflow], enroll_in_sequence); execute() ab args me `_actor` inject karta he. Policy rules_summary me 8th family "workflows" (Rules hub tile -> /dashboard/workflows). TEMPLATES 4 (welcome_new_contact, refund_triage, cod_declined_followup, won_thank_you) - catalog se aate hain, UI hardcode nahi. API (owner-only, API keys 403): GET /portal/workflows/catalog, GET/POST /portal/workflows, GET/PUT/DELETE /portal/workflows/<id> (PUT = new version, DELETE = archive + live runs stop), POST /<id>/status (active|paused; activate needs steps), GET /<id>/runs?limit, POST /<id>/run {conversation_id} (manual run, sync until first wait/approval). WEB: portal.ts PortalWorkflow/WorkflowStep/WorkflowRun/WorkflowCatalog types + 9 helpers (camelCase normalize), lib/omniflow/workflow-payload.ts shared shape guard (route files sirf HTTP exports rakhte hain - Next law), 6 BFF routes (Next 16 `params: Promise` convention - agents/[id] BFF ka latent params bug bhi isi batch me fixed), NEW /dashboard/workflows page: cards (trigger, steps, version, Runs/Live/Goals/Failed, Activate/Pause, Run test with conversation id, Runs timeline with per-step log + open chat, Archive) + form-based step editor (trigger options, stop_on_reply, per-kind config: rule builder with all/any + else control, AI question + fallback, action picker with risk badge + required args + advanced JSON, wait, approval summary, handoff, goal; move up/down; Start from template). D6 visual canvas isi editor model ke upar agla batch he - koi heavy lib nahi. Sidebar (Sell group) + palette + overview quick link (24) + Rules hub icon. ## 197. MASTER-UPGRADE IDENTITY ENGINE (cross-channel identity resolution + duplicate detection + safe reversible merge + audit; pending user run - WEBSITE repo; patcher add_batch_1401_1415_identity_engine.mjs)

Audit-first: identity ka seed pehle se tha - `portal_contact_identities` (portal_contacts.py: `POST /contacts/link` pairwise identity_key, `GET /contacts/identities`) jise profile `linked_channels` aur agent assist parhte hain, aur `POST /customers/merge` (portal_conversations) jo HARD merge he (chats/stages/COD rows physically move). Dono UNTOUCHED (rig pins test_partial_completion/test_customer_360/test_workspace_tools waise hi lock hain). Naya engine unke UPAR he, parallel system nahi: legacy rows ek bar import hoti hain (`migrate_legacy`, per workspace, savepoint-guarded, idempotent - imported rows `idn:<id>` re-key) aur har merge/split ke baad legacy read model sync hota he (`_legacy_sync`) taake profile/assist ko kuch badalna na pare. CP NEW `omniflow-backend-patch/portal_identity.py` (bp `portal_identity`, prefix /api/v1/portal, app.py me register): tables lazy DDL `portal_identities` (display_name, primary_contact_id, status active|merged, merged_into - merged identity history ke liye rehti he) + `portal_identity_handles` (UNIQUE client_id,channel,handle; raw_handle, confidence NUMERIC(4,3), source owner|system|legacy|import|adapter) + `portal_identity_dismissals` (sorted pair, UNIQUE). Channels whatsapp|phone|email|instagram|facebook|tiktok|web|other (OWNER_CHANNELS = whatsapp ke ilawa sab). Resolution = DATA, AI nahi: `normalize_phone` (JID unwrap `@s.whatsapp.net`/`:device`, `@g.us`/`@lid` -> "" (lid -> `lid:<digits>` opaque handle), `00`/`+` strip, local `0`+10-11 digits -> `OF_DEFAULT_COUNTRY_CODE` (default 92), 7-15 digits), `normalize_email` (mailto strip, lower), `normalize_username` (URL/@ strip), `normalize_name` (>=2 words >=5 chars, sirf duplicate hints ke liye). `resolve(cur, client_id, channel, raw, display_name, create=True)` = adapters ka ek call: handle join -> merged_into follow -> (whatsapp/phone) phone alias fallback + self-attach -> warna nayi identity + whatsapp handle + phone alias (confidence 1.0, source system). `merge(keep, merge)` SOFT: handles move, dusri identity `merged` + merged_into (conversations kabhi move nahi hote), blank display_name fill, legacy upsert, audit `identity.merged`. `split(contact)` = reversible: nayi identity + us contact ka whatsapp handle + phone alias move, legacy row delete, audit `identity.split`. `add_handle` -> ok|invalid|limit(`OF_IDENTITY_HANDLES_MAX` 12)|conflict (kisi aur ki handle kabhi steal nahi - 409 + other_contact, UI "Link as same person" offer karti he), `remove_handle` (whatsapp protected -> split use karo), `dismiss` pair. `duplicates(limit, contact)` = conversations scan (`OF_IDENTITY_SCAN_MAX` 3000, GROUP BY contact_id) + handles + dismissals -> same_phone (0.95: ek number do ids ke peeche, e.g. `92300...@s.whatsapp.net` vs `03001234567`) + same_name (0.4), already-linked/dismissed skip, strongest first, `{suggestions, scanned, linked_identities, total}`. API: GET /identity?contact= (any principal; legacy import + resolve + handles + linked names + is contact ke hints; `channels` + `limits.max_handles` bhi) · POST /identity/handles {contact,channel,handle} 200|400|409 identity_conflict · DELETE /identity/handles/<id> 200|404|400 protected · POST /identity/merge {keep,merge} · POST /identity/split {contact} 200|404 · GET /identity/duplicates?limit= · POST /identity/dismiss {contact_a,contact_b}; writes `ensure_human_principal` (API keys read-only). Sab writes `portal_action_log` me (identity.merged/split/handle_added/handle_removed/dismissed). WEB: portal.ts types (CustomerIdentity/IdentityHandle/IdentitySuggestion) + getCustomerIdentity/addIdentityHandle (kind ok|invalid|conflict|unavailable)/removeIdentityHandle/mergeIdentity/splitIdentity/listIdentityDuplicates/dismissIdentityPair; BFF `app/api/omniflow/portal/identity/{route.ts, handles/route.ts, handles/[id]/route.ts (params Promise), merge, split, duplicates, dismiss}` (401/400/404/409/503 mapping); `customers/profile/CustomerIdentityCard.tsx` (Customer 360 me Journey ke neeche: handles list + Remove, Linked contacts + Unlink, "Possibly the same person" hints Link/Not the same person, Add handle form with channel select, 409 -> link offer) + `customers/DuplicatesPanel.tsx` (Customers page: collapsible "Possible duplicates" review - Link as same person / Not the same / Rescan; hard merge per-row UI waisa hi). Deliberately NOT in this batch (slot shifts in pinned suites): brain `read_customer_memory` cross-identity read aur ingest-hook par identity create - adapters batch me fail-soft order ke saath. Rig: test_identity.py 133 checks (normalisation table, resolve/merge/split/handles/duplicates scripted SQL + params, legacy import + savepoint guard, API status codes + auth, BFF depth pins, UI copy/glyph pins). Sweep 141 suites 4943 PASS / 0 FAIL, tsc 0.
